### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An infinite number of straight wires each carrying current <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi></math> are equally placed as shown in the figure. Adjacent wires have current in the opposite direction. The net magnetic field at point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0032924_qUESTION.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 300px; height: 166px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mtext>n</mtext><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mtext>ln</mtext><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mtext>ln</mtext><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** Zero

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mtext>ln</mtext><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>B</mtext><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mi>I</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mo>sin</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mo>sin</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>sin</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8728;</mo></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>cos</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8728;</mo></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

 <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>B</mtext> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>B</mtext> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>k</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>k</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>k</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>k</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>.....</mtext> </mrow> </math>

     <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>sin</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8728;</mo></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>cos</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8728;</mo></msup></mrow></mfrac><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>sin</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8728;</mo></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>cos</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8728;</mo></msup></mrow></mfrac><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>sin</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8728;</mo></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>cos</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8728;</mo></msup></mrow></mfrac><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mtext>.....</mtext></math>

     <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mtext>.....</mtext></math>

     <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mtext>...</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mtext>log</mtext><mtext>e</mtext></msub><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mtext>log</mtext><mtext>e</mtext></msub><mn>2</mn><mover><mo>k</mo><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

    <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mtext>log</mtext><mtext>e</mtext></msub><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

</details>

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Four very long current carrying wires in the same plane intersect to form a square <math><mn>40.0 cm</mn></math> on each side as shown in the diagram. What is the magnitude of current <math><mi>I</mi></math> so that the magnetic field at the centre of the square is zero?

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/xnxfwvjrzuagaxpiawllfnxldtkzplcngiubxxaiqjzkuguhrj.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <mn>22</mn><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>A</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <mn>38</mn><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>A</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow>  <mn>2</mn><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>A</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <mn>18</mn><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>A</mtext></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow>  <mn>2</mn><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>A</mtext></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/kcmsbrnqptaulyezhgioeaqanguvfplwuribblfvuzikanifho.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" />

Magnitude of the magnetic field at a particular distance <math> <mi>x</mi> </math> from infinite long wire is given by

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

For wire <math> <mrow> <mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo></mrow> </math> <math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>20</mn><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> .......(i)

Due to <math> <mrow>  <mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> ......(ii)

Due to <math> <mrow>  <mi>D</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> ......(iii)

Due to <math> <mrow>  <mi>A</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> ......(iv)

From Equations, (i), (ii), (iii) and (iv)

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> [From question]

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>20</mn><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mn>20</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi> </mi><mn>A</mn></math>

(Positive sign shows the direction of current is same direction as that of given direction)

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> is the magnetic field at the centre of a circular loop of area <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and its magnetic dipole moment is found to be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>B</mi><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>m</mi></msup></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>, then what is the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></math>?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let r be the radius of the circular loop.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math></p><p>Magnetic field at the centre of the loop is</p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math></p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math><p>Now, magnetic moment of the loop is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>IA</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac></msqrt><mo>&#183;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math></p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac><mfrac><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup><msqrt><mi>&#960;</mi></msqrt></mfrac></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A conductor wire carrying current <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>i</mi><mo> </mo></math>is placed parallel to a long conducting sheet having a current per unit width <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> and width <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi></math>, as shown in the figure. the force per unit length on the conductor wire will be

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/embip.jpg" style="width: 250px; height: 157px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace></mspace><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mi>π</mi></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext><mrow><mtext>2</mtext><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mi>&#x3C0;</mi></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext></mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mspace/><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>&#x3C0;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow><mi>d</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mspace/><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>&#x3C0;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace></mspace><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mi>π</mi></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext><mrow><mtext>2</mtext><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/10769sol.PNG" style="width: 246px; height: 158px;" />​

<mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">F</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mo>∫</mo> <mtext mathvariant="italic">dF</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtex>sin <mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi> </mtex></mrow>

  <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mo>∫</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mi mathvariant="italic">π</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">j</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">dx</mtext> <mtext mathvariant="italic">.</mtext> <mtext mathvariant="italic">i</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <msqrt> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext mathvariant="italic">.</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi> </mrow> </math>

  <math> <semantics> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mi mathvariant="italic">π</mi> </mfrac> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">j</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">i</mi><msup> <mstyle displaystyle="true" mathsize="140%"><mo>∫</mo></mstyle> <mtext mathvariant="italic">​</mtext> </msup> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi mathvariant="italic">d</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">x</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <msqrt> <mrow> <msup> <mi mathvariant="italic">h</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo><msup> <mi mathvariant="italic">x</mi> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mrow></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> <mi mathvariant="italic">·</mi><mfrac> <mi mathvariant="italic">h</mi> <mrow> <msqrt> <mrow> <msup> <mi mathvariant="italic">h</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo><msup> <mi mathvariant="italic">x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <annotation encoding="MathType-MTEF">MathType@MTEF@5@5@+=feaaguart1ev2aaatCvAUfeBSjuyZL2yd9gzLbvyNv2CaerbuLwBLnhiov2DGi1BTfMBaeXatLxBI9gBaerbd9wDYLwzYbItLDharqqtubsr4rNCHbGeaGqiVu0Je9sqqrpepC0xbbL8F4rqqrFfpeea0xe9Lq=Jc9vqaqpepm0xbba9pwe9Q8fs0=yqaqpepae9pg0FirpepeKkFr0xfr=xfr=xb9adbaqaaeGaciGaaiaabeqaamaabaabaaGcbaaeaaaaaaaaa8qacqGH9aqpdaWcaaWdaeaapeGaeqiVd02damaaBaaaleaapeGaaGimaaWdaeqaaaGcbaWdbiabec8aWbaacaWGQbWdamaaBaaaleaapeGaaGimaaWdaeqaaOWdbiaadMgapaWaaubiaeqaleqabaGaaGzaVdqdbaWdbiabgUIiYdaakmaalaaapaqaa8qacaWGKbGaamiEaaWdaeaapeWaaOaaa8aabaWdbiaadIgapaWaaWbaaSqabeaapeGaaGOmaaaakiabgUcaRiaadIhapaWaaWbaaSqabeaapeGaaGOma8aadaahaaadbeqaaaaaaaaal8qabeaaaaGccaGG3cWaaSaaa8aabaWdbiaadIgaa8aabaWdbmaakaaapaqaa8qacaWGObWdamaaCaaaleqabaWdbiaaikdaaaGccqGHRaWkcaWG4bWdamaaCaaaleqabaWdbiaaikdaaaaabeaaaaaaaa@51FD@</annotation> </semantics> </math>

 <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mi mathvariant="italic">π</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">j</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">i</mtext> <mo>·</mo> </msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <munderover> <mo>∫</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext> <mo>/</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">dx</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

 <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mi mathvariant="italic">π</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">j</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> <msubsup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">1</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <msup> <mtext>tan</mtext> <mn>-1</mn> </msup> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">x</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext> <mo>/</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mover> <mtext mathvariant="italic">F</mtext> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">j</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">i</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mi mathvariant="italic">π</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msup> <mtext>tan</mtext> <mn>-1</mn> </msup> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">2h</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext mathvariant="italic">k</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A current <math><mi>i</mi></math> is flowing through the loop. The direction of the current and the shape of the loop are as shown in the figure. The magnetic field at the centre of the loop is
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>MA</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>MB</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>∠</mi><mi>DMA</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>90</mn><mo>°</mo><mo>)</mo></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/kanu.jpg" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" style="width: 220px; height: 204px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> but out of the plane of the paper
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <mfrac>   <mn>5</mn>   <mrow>    <mn>16</mn></mrow>  </mfrac>  <mfrac>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mi>&#x03BC;</mi>     <mn>0</mn>    </msub>    <mi>i</mi></mrow>   <mi>R</mi>  </mfrac>  <mo>,</mo></mrow></math> but into the plane of the paper
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> but out of the plane of the paper
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> but into the plane of the paper

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> but into the plane of the paper**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>(i) The magnetic field at the centre due to the curved portion <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>DA</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math>

According to the right-hand screw rule, the magnetic field will be into the plane of the paper.

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/asa.jpg" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" style="width: 200px; height: 197px;" />

(ii) The magnetic field at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> due to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AB</mi></math> is zero.

(iii) The magnetic field at the centre due to the curved portion <math><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>According to the right-hand screw rule, the magnetic field will be into the plane of the paper.

(iv) The magnetic field at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> due to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>DC</mi></math> is zero.

Hence, the resultant magnetic field at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>s</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi><mi>i</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>p</mi></mrow></msub><mi>&#160;</mi><mo></mo></math>

Both are perpendicular downward

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#10754;</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A long wire carries a steady current. It is bent into a circle of one turn and the magnetic field at the center of the coil is <math><mi>B</mi></math>. It is then bent into a circular loop of <math><mi>n</mi></math> turns. The magnetic field at the centre of coil will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>n</mi><mi>B</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>B</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Suppose the length of wire is <math><mi>l</mi></math>

Then, for the circle of n turns

The magnetic field, <math> <mrow> <mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi>I</mi><mi>n</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> , where R is the radius of the circle

Then <math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> =B......(i)

Here, <math><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>l</mi></math> .......(ii)

Now, if the same wire is turned into a coil of n turns of radius a then,

<math><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>×</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>l</mi></math> .......(iii)

Thus, <math><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>×</mo><mi>n</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

Now, the magnetic field for the coil of n turns, is

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mi>n</mi></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>∵</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

At the centre of a current-carrying circular coil of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>cm</mi></math>, magnetic field due to earth is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>. The current flowing through the coil, so that it equals the Earth's magnetic field, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>40</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.4</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.2</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.4</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Magnetic field <math><mo>(</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo></math> at the centre of a coil of radius <math><mo>(</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>)</mo></math> carrying current <math><mo>(</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is

<math><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>

Given, <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.05</mn><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<math><mi> </mi><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mn>0.05</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.398</mn><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>≈</mo><mn>0.4</mn><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A disc of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math> rotates with a constant angular velocity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></math> about its own axis. The surface charge density of this disc varies as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">σ</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>ar</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math> is the distance from the centre of the disc. Determine the magnetic field intensity at the centre of the disc.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>a&#x3C9;R</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>a&#x3C9;R</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>a&#x3C9;R</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow><mn>8</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>a&#x3C9;R</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>a&#x3C9;R</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/Val.PNG" style="width: 200px; height: 267px;" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mtext>dA</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mtext>r</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mtext>dr</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mtext>dq</mtext><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#963;</mi><mo>&#183;</mo><mtext>dA</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;a</mi><msup><mtext>r</mtext><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mtext>dr</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mtext>i</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mtext>dq</mtext></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mtext>dq</mtext></mfenced><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>a&#969;</mi><msup><mtext>r</mtext><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mtext>dr</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mtext>dB</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>i</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext>r</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>a&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><msup><mtext>r</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mtext>dr</mtext></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mtext>B</mtext><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mtext>R</mtext></munderover><mtext>dB</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>a&#969;R</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Two infinite sheets carrying current in same direction (of equal current per unit length <math> <mtext>K</mtext> </math> ) are separated by a distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>d</mtext></math>. A proton is released from a point between the plates with a velocity parallel to the sheets but perpendicular to the direction of current in the sheets. Then the path of the proton is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0065303_Ques.jpg" style="width: 188px; height: 81px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** circle
- **(B)** helix
- **(C)** straight line
- **(D)** straight line only if it is released from a point exactly midway between the two plates.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) straight line**

**Detailed Solution:**

As net magnetic field due to sheets will be zero, net force acting on the proton also becomes zero, it will continue to move on a straight line. <math> <mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>∵ </mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>F</mtext> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>q</mtext> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>V</mtext> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>×</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>B </mtext> <mo>→ </mo> </mover></mrow></mfenced> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A battery is connected between two points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> on the circumference of a uniform conducting ring of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math> and resistance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math>. One of the arcs <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AB</mi></math> of the ring subtends an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math> at the centre. The value of the magnetic induction at the centre due to the current in the ring is

**Options:**

- **(A)** proportional to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>180</mn><mo>°</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** inversely proportional to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>
- **(C)** zero, only if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>180</mn><mo>°</mo><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** zero for all values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) zero for all values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For a current flowing into a circular arc, the magnetic induction at the centre is

          <math> <mrow> <mtext>B</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>i</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mi>&#960;</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <mi>&#160;&#952;</mi> </mrow> </mrow> </math>

or          <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>B</mtext><mo>&#8733;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></math>

In the given problem, the total current is divided into two arcs

         <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mo>&#8733;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mtext>resistance of arc</mtext></mfrac></math>

         <math> <mrow> <mo>&#8733;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>length of arc</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

          <math> <mrow> <mo>&#8733;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>angle subtended at centre&#160;</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mi>&#952;</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>


<u><strong><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0007049_AnsExp4_1.jpg" style="width: 224px; height: 226px;" /></strong></u>


or          <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>i</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mtext>constant</mtext></math>

i.e., magnetic field at centre due to arc <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AB</mi></math> is equal and opposite to the magnetic field at centre due to arc <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>ACB</mi></math>. Or the net magnetic field at the centre is zero.

</details>

---

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A conductor wire carrying current <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>i</mi><mo> </mo></math>is placed symmetrically and parallel to a long conducting sheet having a current per unit width <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> and width <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi></math>, as shown in the figure. the force per unit length on the conductor wire will be

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/embip.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 240px; height: 150px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace></mspace><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mi>π</mi></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext><mrow><mtext>2</mtext><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mi>&#x3C0;</mi></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext></mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mspace/><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>&#x3C0;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow><mi>d</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mspace/><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>&#x3C0;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace></mspace><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi>j</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mi>π</mi></mfrac><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext><mrow><mtext>2</mtext><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/10769sol.PNG" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 246px; height: 158px;" />​

<mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">F</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mo>&int;</mo> <mtext mathvariant="italic">dF</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtex>sin <mi mathvariant="normal">&theta;</mi> </mtex></mrow>

  <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mo>&#8747;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">&#956;</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mi mathvariant="italic">&#960;</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">j</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">dx</mtext> <mtext mathvariant="italic">.</mtext> <mtext mathvariant="italic">i</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <msqrt> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext mathvariant="italic">.</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi> </mrow> </math>

  <math> <semantics> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">&#956;</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mi mathvariant="italic">&#960;</mi> </mfrac> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">j</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">i</mi><msup> <mstyle displaystyle="true" mathsize="140%"><mo>&#8747;</mo></mstyle> <mtext mathvariant="italic">&#8203;</mtext> </msup> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi mathvariant="italic">d</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">x</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <msqrt> <mrow> <msup> <mi mathvariant="italic">h</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo><msup> <mi mathvariant="italic">x</mi> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mrow></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> <mi mathvariant="italic">&#183;</mi><mfrac> <mi mathvariant="italic">h</mi> <mrow> <msqrt> <mrow> <msup> <mi mathvariant="italic">h</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo><msup> <mi mathvariant="italic">x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <annotation encoding="MathType-MTEF">MathType@MTEF@5@5@+=feaaguart1ev2aaatCvAUfeBSjuyZL2yd9gzLbvyNv2CaerbuLwBLnhiov2DGi1BTfMBaeXatLxBI9gBaerbd9wDYLwzYbItLDharqqtubsr4rNCHbGeaGqiVu0Je9sqqrpepC0xbbL8F4rqqrFfpeea0xe9Lq=Jc9vqaqpepm0xbba9pwe9Q8fs0=yqaqpepae9pg0FirpepeKkFr0xfr=xfr=xb9adbaqaaeGaciGaaiaabeqaamaabaabaaGcbaaeaaaaaaaaa8qacqGH9aqpdaWcaaWdaeaapeGaeqiVd02damaaBaaaleaapeGaaGimaaWdaeqaaaGcbaWdbiabec8aWbaacaWGQbWdamaaBaaaleaapeGaaGimaaWdaeqaaOWdbiaadMgapaWaaubiaeqaleqabaGaaGzaVdqdbaWdbiabgUIiYdaakmaalaaapaqaa8qacaWGKbGaamiEaaWdaeaapeWaaOaaa8aabaWdbiaadIgapaWaaWbaaSqabeaapeGaaGOmaaaakiabgUcaRiaadIhapaWaaWbaaSqabeaapeGaaGOma8aadaahaaadbeqaaaaaaaaal8qabeaaaaGccaGG3cWaaSaaa8aabaWdbiaadIgaa8aabaWdbmaakaaapaqaa8qacaWGObWdamaaCaaaleqabaWdbiaaikdaaaGccqGHRaWkcaWG4bWdamaaCaaaleqabaWdbiaaikdaaaaabeaaaaaaaa@51FD@</annotation> </semantics> </math>

 <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">&#956;</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mi mathvariant="italic">&#960;</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">j</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">i</mtext> <mo>&#183;</mo> </msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <munderover> <mo>&#8747;</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext> <mo>/</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </munderover> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">dx</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext mathvariant="italic">x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

 <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi mathvariant="italic">&#956;</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mi mathvariant="italic">&#960;</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">j</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> <msubsup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">1</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <msup> <mtext>tan</mtext> <mn>-1</mn> </msup> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">x</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext> <mo>/</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mtext mathvariant="italic">F</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">j</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">i</mtext></mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac><msup><mtext>tan</mtext><mn>-1</mn></msup><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">d</mtext><mrow><mtext>2</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext mathvariant="italic">k</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q48. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A conductor is carrying a current <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math>. The magnetic field intensity at the point <math> <mtext>O</mtext> </math> which is the common centre for three arcs is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/78958_q.png" style="width: 170px; height: 200px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">µ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>iθ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn><mi>πR</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#181;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn><mi>&#960;R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#181;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn><mi>&#960;R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** zero

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">µ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>iθ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn><mi>πR</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Find <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> due to each arc at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> and add them using vector addition.

Since magnetic field at the centre of an arc is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">µ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>πr</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>

hence, net <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">µ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">µ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>iθ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn><mi>πR</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Two concentric coils of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math> turns each are placed in the same plane. Their radii are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mo> </mo><mi>cm</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>40</mn><mo> </mo><mi>cm</mi></math> and carry  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>  and  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> current respectively in opposite directions. The magnetic induction (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>tesla</mi></math>) at the center is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Two coils carry currents in opposite directions, hence net magnetic field at centre will be difference of the two fields.

<math><mi>i</mi><mi>e</mi><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mo>.</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>N</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An infinitely long hollow conducting cylinder with inner radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> and outer radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> carries a uniform current density along its length. The magnitude of the magnetic field, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced></math> as a function of the radial distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> from the axis, is best represented by:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/321375a2-34b7-4fab-806d-bba574c7715d.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 119px; width: 150px;" />
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/20ded29f-09df-4fc9-9bf7-c48cd6ad7de9.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 110px; width: 150px;" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/f9f3b0f5-fafe-47a7-8bf8-eaa81dabf9d6.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 113px; width: 150px;" />
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/2859c302-c360-4548-b50d-21ad9aa128ff.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 116px; width: 150px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/2859c302-c360-4548-b50d-21ad9aa128ff.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 116px; width: 150px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mfrac><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> , <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>R</mi></math> , <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>R</mi></math> , <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math>

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/2859c302-c360-4548-b50d-21ad9aa128ff.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 116px; width: 150px;" />

</details>

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> is the magnetic field at the centre of a circular loop of area <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and its magnetic dipole moment is found to be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>B</mi><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>m</mi></msup></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>, then what is the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></math>?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let r be the radius of the circular loop.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math></p><p>Magnetic field at the centre of the loop is</p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math></p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math><p>Now, magnetic moment of the loop is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>IA</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mfrac></msqrt><mo>&#183;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math></p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac><mfrac><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup><msqrt><mi>&#960;</mi></msqrt></mfrac></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A current of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is flowing in the sides of an equilateral triangle of side <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn><mo> </mo><mi>cm</mi></math>. The magnetic field at the centroid of the triangle is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.66</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.22</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.33</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.44</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.33</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The magnetic field at the centre O due to the current through side AB is

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/mrfhtjjenwbvgbikpvqklziaimgcdbhdqeycuqiptspbhqciic.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" />

As the magnetic field due to each of the three sides is the same in magnitude and direction. So, the total magnetic field at O is sum of all the fields.

i.e. <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>

Here, <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

Now <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></math> <math><mo>[</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1.33</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>

</details>
