### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A current-carrying wire of certain length is bent to form an arc that subtends an angle <math><mi>θ</mi></math> at the centre. Let <math><mi>B</mi></math> be the magnetic field at the centre due to this wire. The correct graph between <math><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0375038_opt_1.JPG" style="width: 130px; height: 121px;" />
- **(B)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0375038_opt_2.JPG" style="width: 130px; height: 124px;" />
- **(C)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0375038_opt_3.JPG" style="width: 130px; height: 120px;" />
- **(D)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0375038_opt_4.JPG" style="width: 130px; height: 115px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0375038_opt_2.JPG" style="width: 130px; height: 124px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>θ</mi></math>

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>i</mi><mi>l</mi><msup><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>∝</mo><msup><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Find the magnetic induction at point O, if the current carrying wire is in the shape shown in the figure.

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/KT0000020_Ques_1_1.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 150px; height: 86px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>&mu;</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>I</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> <mi>&pi;</mi> <mtext>r</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mi>&pi;</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⊙</mo> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>&mu;</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>I</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mi>&pi;</mi> <mtext>r</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mi>&pi;</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⊙</mo> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>I</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> <mtext>r</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac>  <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⊙</mo> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>I</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mi>π</mi> <mtext>r</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⊙</mo> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>I</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mi>π</mi> <mtext>r</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mi>π</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⊙</mo> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mtext>,</mtext></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mtext>I</mtext><mtext>r</mtext></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8857;</mo></math>,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mtext>I</mtext><mtext>r</mtext></mfrac><mo>&#8857;</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>I</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mtext>r</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8857;</mo></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/10584s.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 229px; height: 149px;" />

</details>

---

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The magnetic field at the point of intersection of diagonals of a square loop of side <math><mi>L</mi></math> carrying a current <math><mi>I</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>From Biot-Savart&#39;s law, magnetic field due to current carrying conductor is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mi>i</mi><mi>r</mi></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#981;</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#981;</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>

Where, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#981;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>45</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#981;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>45</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>.</mo><mfrac><mi>i</mi><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mi>L</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mstyle></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

Since there are four sides, the total magnetic field is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>net</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A tightly wound solenoid of radius <math><mi>‘</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>’</mi></math> and length <math><mi>‘</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>’</mi></math> has <em>n</em> turns per unit length. It carries an electric current <em>i</em>. Magnetic field at a distance <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> from one of the end (inside the solenoid on its axis) is <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>n</mi><mi>i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi>K</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> for <math><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi></math> . Then find the value of <em>K</em>.

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/Phys_21.JPG" style="width: 250px; height: 103px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **40**

**Detailed Solution:**

Hint: <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>n</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>s</mi><mi>i</mi><mi>n</mi><msub><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>s</mi><mi>i</mi><mi>n</mi><msub><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0bc5803f-4230-4093-a611-8c89c8f3a061-image29.png" style="height: 123px; width: 250px;" />

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>n</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>n</mi><mi>i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>40</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi>K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>40</mn></math>

</details>

### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Four very long wires are arranged as shown in the figure, so that their cross-section forms a square, with connections at the ends so that current <math><mi>I</mi></math> flow through all four wires. Length of each side of the formed such square is <math><mi>b</mi></math>. The magnetic field at the central point <math><mi>P</mi></math> (centre of the square) is

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/vybdjdrehjeqvpfrqvrlbiysrtronopmjpdatqlamsjpyhpeiu.png" style="height: 85px; width: 280px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi> </mi><mi>π</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/jee_phys2.JPG" style="width: 180px; height: 172px;" />

net field <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi> </mi><mi>B</mi></math>

where <math> <mrow> <mi>B</mi><mtext> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi>I</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>b</mi><mtext> </mtext><msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi></math> net field <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Current is flowing in conducting wire shaped as shown in the figure. The radius of the curved part is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> and the length of straight portion is very large. The value of the magnetic field at the centre <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi></math> is <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>, where the value of <math><mi>k</mi></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/D-9_que.png" style="width: 250px; height: 104px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>B</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>I</mi></mrow><mi>R</mi></mfrac></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/D-10_solu.png" style="width: 220px; height: 108px;" />

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>κ</mi><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

When <math><mi>κ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q59. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A current-carrying wire is placed in the grooves of an insulating semicircular disc of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math>, as shown. The current enters at point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and leaves from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math>. Determine the magnetic field at point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math>.

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0032889_Question.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 180px; height: 124px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** none of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0032889_ANs.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 200px; height: 132px;" /></p><p>The angle formed by lines from each end of diameter to any point of the semicircle is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>90</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math>.</p><p>Therefore, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>CB</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8869;</mo><mi>DB</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>CA</mi><mo>&#8869;</mo><mi>DA</mi></math>.</p><p>
Magnetic field due to wire <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AC</mi></math>,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>AC</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi><mo>sin</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mo>cos</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>cos</mo><mn>90</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>
Magnetic field due to wire <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>BC</mi></math>,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>BC</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo mathvariant="italic">R</mo><mo>sin</mo><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mo>cos</mo><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>cos</mo><mn>90</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">I</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">I</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mover><mtext mathvariant="italic">B</mtext><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>net</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">B</mtext><mtext>A</mtext></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">B</mtext><mtext>B</mtext></msub></math>

       <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

       <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn mathvariant="italic">0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q60. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>The figure shows a long coaxial cable in which a current <math><mi>I</mi></math> flows down through the inner cylinder of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math> and the same current flows back up through the outer cylinder of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></math>. The cylinders are insulated from each other and the current is uniformly distributed over the area of the cross-section in each cylinder. The strength of the magnetic field at a distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> from the axis of the cable is</p><p>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/3199f0a9-8cb8-41bf-af02-a87a12bea416-image6.jpeg" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>36</mn><mi>&#x3C0;</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn><mi>&#x3C0;</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn><mi>&#x3C0;</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>36</mn><mi>&#x3C0;</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>36</mn><mi>&#x3C0;</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Using ampere&#39;s law for a closed loop of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> around the axis of the cable,</p><p><math open="[" close="]"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>I</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mfenced open="[" ### Q61. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A finite carrying-current wire is placed in a plane along with a very long, straight conductor carrying current as shown in the figure. The torque on the finite current wire about point <em><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math></em> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/WhatsApp_Image_2020-06-15_at_3.28.02_PM.jpeg" style="width: 200px; height: 149px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>dF</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>dx</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>dF</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac><mi>dx</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>d&#964;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>dF</mi><mo>&#8901;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>dx</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#964;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><munderover><mo>&#8747;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></munderover><mi>dx</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>

</details>

### Q62. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A very long cylindrical wire is carrying a current <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msub></math> distributed uniformly over its cross-section area. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> is the centre of the cross-section of the wire and the direction of current in into the plane of the figure. The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mo>∫</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></msubsup><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>dI</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> along the path <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AB</mi></math> (from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math>) is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image18-79aaaebc-535a-455e-9ea2-d1e6f3a28208.jpg" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>According to Ampere&#39;s circuital law</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8750;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>dl</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mi>net</mi></msub></math></p><p>For the closed path <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>OAB</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8750;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>dl</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></msubsup><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>dl</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></msubsup><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>dl</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></msubsup><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>dl</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mn>6</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Since the magnetic field is perpendicular to the paths <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>OA</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>BO</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></msubsup><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>dl</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

The magnetic induction at point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is</p><p>
<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image23-672f9962-bc9f-43be-b65a-fe8f3adbbcdb.jpg" style="height: 180px; width: 200px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image24-a2ddd510-7625-412f-abcd-f687973977b9.jpg" style="height: 128px; width: 200px;" />

There is no magnetic field at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> due to wire in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">→</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><msub><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q64. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The magnitude of the magnetic field at the centre of an equilateral triangular loop of side <math><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> which is carrying a current of <math><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is:
[Take <math><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> ]

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>18</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>9</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>18</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/d61b559a-60b7-43f9-992a-b79c18490ebb-image17.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 179px; width: 180px;" />

The perpendicular distance of centroid from any side, <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
Magnetic field due to one side.
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">µ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>]</mo></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><msup><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>°</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><msup><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>°</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>15</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
Net magnetic field <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>45</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>45</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>180</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>18</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>

</details>

### Q65. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A long thin non-conducting cylindrical pipe of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> carrying a uniform surface charge density <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>σ</mi></math> is rotating about its axis with a constant angular speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>ω</mi></math>. The energy density of the magnetic field inside the tube is

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/9e9dabee-bb29-43a8-8827-21ddcb17c89e-image14.png" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>&#x3C3;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>&#x3C9;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>R</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>&#x3C3;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>&#x3C9;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>&#x3C3;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>&#x3C9;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>&#x3C3;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>&#x3C9;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>R</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>&#x3C3;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>&#x3C9;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The rotating cylinder is equivalent to a solenoid with current per unit length <math><mo>=</mo><mi>&#963;</mi><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>R</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>&#963;</mi><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>R</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><msubsup><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#183;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#963;</mi><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>&#963;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q66. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The figure shows a current-carrying loop, some part of which is circular and some part is a line segment. The magnetic induction at the centre is 

<u><strong><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0013766_Ques1_1.jpg" style="width: 174px; height: 151px;" /></strong></u>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>&#956;</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>I&#960;</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mtext>μ</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>I</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mtext>π</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mtext>π</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mtext>&#956;</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>I</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mtext>&#960;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mtext>3μ</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>I</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mtext>3μ</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>I</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The magnetic field due to circular arc is

​      <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>B</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mtext>μ</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>I</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mtext>π</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mtext>μ</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>I</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mtext>π</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mtext>π</mtext></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mtext>μ</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>I</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q67. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

In the given figure magnetic field at the center of ring <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>. Now it is turned through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>90</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math> about <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>xx</mi><mo>'</mo></math> axis, so that two semicircular parts are mutually perpendicular. The new value of magnetic field (in Tesla) at centre is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt></math>. Find <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math>.


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/630123-2.JPG" style="width: 500px; height: 200px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **16**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math></p><p>In 2<sup>nd</sup> case,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q68. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A symmetric star shaped conducting wire loop is carrying a steady state current <math> <mi>I</mi> </math> as shown in the figure. The distance between the diametrically opposite vertices of the star is <math><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi></math> .

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/190774q.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 196px; height: 169px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mn>3</mn><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mn>6</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mn>6</mn><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mn>3</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mn>6</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The given points (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6) makes 360<sup>o</sup> angle at ‘O’. Hence angle made by vertices 1 and 2 with ‘O’ is 60<sup>o</sup>.</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/190774s.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 319px; height: 226px;" /></p><p>Direction of magnetic field at ‘O’ due to each segment is same. Since it is symmetric star shape, magnitude will also be same.</p><p>Magnetic field due to section BC.</p><p><math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>60°</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>30°</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>×</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>k</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q69. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Two long parallel wires carry currents <math><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> such that <math><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>.</mo></math> When the currents are in the same direction, the magnetic field at a point midway between the wires is <math><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>.</mo></math> If the direction of <math><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is reversed, the field becomes <math><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>.</mo></math> The ratio <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

When the currents are in the same direction then the magnetic field

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>d</mi></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>i</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>d</mi></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>i</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

When the currents are in the reversed direction then the magnetic field

<math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>d</mi></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>i</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>d</mi></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>i</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

Dividing Eq. (i) by Eq. (ii)

<math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

or <math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

or <math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>5</mn><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

or <math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q70. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A cylindrical cavity of diameter a exists inside a cylinder of diameter <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></math> as shown in the figure. Both the cylinder and the cavity are infinity long. A uniform current density <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>J</mi></math> flows along the length. If the magnitude of the magnetic field at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">N</mtext><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mi>μ</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>a</mi><mtext mathvariant="italic">J</mtext></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0012130_Ques1_1.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 250px; height: 197px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtext>B</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>J</mtext> <mi>π</mi> <msup> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>J</mtext> <mi>π</mi> <msup> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>/</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>π</mi> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>B</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> <mi>μ</mi> </mrow> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>J</mtext> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mtext>N</mtext> <mtext>J</mtext> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>So</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>N</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>5</mn></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q71. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A wire is bent in the form of a circular arc of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> with a straight portion <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math>. If the current in the wire is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>i</mi></math>, then the magnetic induction at point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/WhatsApp_Image_2020-05-23_at_8.40.56_PM.jpeg" style="width: 205px; height: 165px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3D5;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3D5;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#x2061;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3D5;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#x2061;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3D5;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3BC;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3C0;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3D5;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#x2061;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#x3D5;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">ϕ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>

Where <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math> <mtable columnalign="left"> <mtr> <mtd> <mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi>i</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>tan</mi><mi>ϕ</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>μ</mi> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mi>i</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>−</mo><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>tan</mi><mi>ϕ</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </math>

</details>

---
